## `AFG-4773-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** UV-Spectrophotometer Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Spectacle Lens Focal Power Lensmeter & Prism Deviation Quality
- **Intro:** UV-Spectrophotometer Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Spectacle Lens Focal Power Lensmeter & Prism Deviation Quality — Pre-Calibration Environment and Instrument Readiness Check: Temperature: 23±1°C; Humidity: 50±5% RH; Vibration: <0.1g; Mains voltage: 230 VAC ±10%.

- **Standards:** `ISO 8598:2014`, `ISO/IEC 17025:2017`, `ANSI Z80.1-2015 American National Standard for Ophthalmics - Pre`, `Law 2016`
